These are all the changes included in the Home Assistant 2026.12 release.
For a summary in a more readable format
[Release notes blog for this release](/blog/2026/12/02/release-202612/).

## All changes

- [Backend](#backend)
- [Frontend](#frontend)

- Flatten the Status filter on the entities page ([@timmo001] - [frontend#56114])
- Use the open tilt position and slat angles of covers ([@piitaya] - [frontend#56090])
- Update dependency lint-staged to v17.6.2 (@renovatebot - [frontend#56128])
- Make one slanted wave the default skeleton effect ([@timmo001] - [frontend#56089])
- Move database calculate into card ([@erwindouna] - [frontend#56082])
- Reset the circular slider when a touch is cancelled after a press ([@martin-s-a] - [frontend#56127])
- Fix light card errors when dragging the slider of an unknown light ([@martin-s-a] - [frontend#56129])
- Read localize from the filters' i18n context ([@maartenla] - [frontend#56118])
- Draw the power sources graph zero line above all series ([@MindFreeze] - [frontend#56000])
- Keep short timeline state changes from merging into neighbours of the same state ([@MindFreeze] - [frontend#56131])
- Allow requiring client certificates in Link remote connection ([@javitonino] - [frontend#56093])
- Make the camera more info as wide as its picture fits ([@wendevlin] - [frontend#56109])
- Show automation validation errors on the rows they belong to ([@wendevlin] - [frontend#55830])
- Translate automation validation errors and show them at more fields ([@wendevlin] - [frontend#56111])
- Pick the automation sidebar from the row that opened it ([@maartenla] - [frontend#55986])
- Show the debug logging option on the page of an integration that is not set up ([@gjohansson-ST] - [frontend#56124])
- Send slider keyboard changes when focus leaves before the key is released ([@martin-s-a] - [frontend#56132])
- Add device or integration dialog with categories, brands and search ([@frenck] - [frontend#55125])
- Move the camera microphone row height to its more info ([@wendevlin] - [frontend#56137])
- Add timer list card and more info dialog ([@balloob] - [frontend#56099])
- Save the add dialog's Browse by choice only from its chip ([@piitaya] - [frontend#56140])
- Keep the startup toast usable while a dialog is open ([@wendevlin] - [frontend#56134])
- Add address search to location selector ([@Suryeah] - [frontend#54351])
- Update pnpm to v12.23.0 (@renovatebot - [frontend#56138])
- Show integration categories in the Marketplace ([@frenck] - [frontend#55130])
